Let's draft code skeleton.

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _fft_len(L):
    size=1
    while size < L: size <<=1
    return size

def build_anti_h(x,N):
    h=np.empty(2*N, dtype=float)
    h[:N]=x
    h[N:]=1.0 - x[::-1]
    return h

def correlate_anti(x,N):
    n=2*N
    h=build_anti_h(x,N)
    b=1.0-h
    L=2*N-1
    size=1 << (L-1).bit_length()  # if L>0
    f1=np.fft.rfft(h[::-1], n=size)
    f2=np.fft.rfft(b, n=size)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(f1*f2, n=size)[:L]
    return corr * (1.0/N)

def c5_anti(x,N):
    c=correlate_anti(x,N)
    return float(c.max())
```
Check `bit_length`: for L=1, L-1=0 bit_length=0, 1<<0=1; while? Actually 1 << (L-1).bit_length() gives smallest power >=L? For L=8, L-1=7 bit_length=3, 1<<3=8. For L=9, 8 bit_length=4, 16. Good.

But `np.fft.rfft(h[::-1])` copies. Fine.

Could compute b before? okay.

`select_active`:
```
def select_active(c, top_k=48, abs_tol=1e-7, rel_tol=5e-4):
    n=len(c)//2+1? Actually L=2N-1, n=(L+1)//2
    maxv=float(c.max())
    thresh=maxv - max(abs_tol, rel_tol*maxv)
    cand=np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
    if cand.size ==0: cand=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    if cand.size > top_k:
       order=np.argsort(-c[cand])
       cand=cand[order[:top_k]]
    # add opposite lags
    n=(len(c)+1)//2
    extras=[]
    for idx in cand:
       opp=2*n-2-int(idx)
       if opp>=0 and opp<len(c) and (opp not in cand): extras.append(opp)
    if extras:
       active=np.unique(np.concatenate((cand, np.array(extras))))
       # if too many, sort by c and keep top top_k*2 maybe
       if active.size > max(2*top_k, 32):
           active=active[np.argsort(-c[active])[:max(2*top_k,32)]]
    else:
       active=cand
    return active
```
Using `opp not in cand` O(k^2) small. Could use set but simpler.

`stable_logsum`:
```
def logsumexp(vals, alpha):
    a=alpha*vals
    mx=a.max()
    if np.isfinite(mx):
       return float(mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(a-mx))))
    return float(vals.max())
```
But objective wants m + logsum(alpha*(vals-m))/alpha. We'll implement inline.

`active_obj`:
```
def active_obj(x,N,active,alpha):
    c=correlate_anti(x,N)
    vals=c[active]
    m=vals.max()
    sh=alpha*(vals-m)
    mx=sh.max()
    val=m + (mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-mx))))/alpha
    return float(val)
```
Memory c length 2N, okay.

`active_grad` as above. Need active may be np array. Weights compute:
```
def active_grad(x,N,active,alpha):
    c=correlate_anti(x,N)
    vals=c[active]
    m=vals.max()
    sh=alpha*(vals-m)
    mx=sh.max()
    w=np.exp(sh-mx); w/=w.sum()
    n=2*N; h=build_anti_h(x,N); dx=1.0/N
    g=np.zeros(n)
    center=n-1
    for idx,wi in zip(active,w):
       if wi <= 1e-15: continue
       k=int(idx)-center
       wm=wi*dx
       if k>=0:
          nv=n-k
          g[0:nv] += wm*(1.0-h[k:n])
          g[k:n] += wm*(-h[0:nv])
       else:
          kk=-k
          nv=n-kk
          g[kk:n] += wm*(1.0-h[0:nv])
          g[0:nv] += wm*(-h[kk:n])
    gx = g[:N] - g[n-1:N-1:-1]
    return gx
```
Can be optimized by precomputing h. For k=0, g[0:n] updates twice. Good.
Potential issue: weights normalized but objective smooth m + logsum/alpha; gradient formula weights w normalized (softmax) is correct: derivative = ∑ w ∂c.

`optimize_lbfgs`:
```
def optimize_lbfgs(x,N,deadline,alphas,iters,top_k=48):
    best_x=x.copy(); best_c=c5_anti(best_x,N)
    cur_x=best_x.copy()
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*N
    for round_idx in range(2):
      if time.time() > deadline: break
      for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
        remaining=deadline-time.time()
        if remaining < 20: break
        c=correlate_anti(cur_x,N); active=select_active(c, top_k=top_k)
        maxit = iters
        # scale maxit by N? lower N more
        maxfun = maxit*8
        try:
          res=minimize(active_obj, cur_x, jac=active_grad, args=(N, active, alpha), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxit, 'maxfun':maxfun, 'ftol':1e-20, 'gtol':1e-16, 'maxls':50, 'maxcor':50})
          xnew=np.clip(res.x,0,1)
        except Exception:
          continue
        cnew=c5_anti(xnew,N)
        if cnew < best_c:
           best_c=cnew; best_x=xnew.copy()
        if cnew <= best_c + 1e-12:
           cur_x=xnew.copy()
        else:
           cur_x=best_x.copy()
    return best_x,best_c
```
But if best_c updated, `cnew <= best_c + 1e-12` cur. If cnew slightly worse than best but improved from cur? Not. Could allow if cnew <= max(best_c, c_cur_old +1e-6). Need old c. Simpler: if cnew < best_c or cnew < c_cur - 1e-12: cur=xnew. We'll track cur_c.
```
cur_c=c5_anti(cur_x,N)
...
if cnew < best_c: update
if cnew <= max(best_c, cur_c) + 1e-12: cur=xnew; cur_c=cnew
else: cur=best; cur_c=best_c
```
Good.

Do two rounds maybe time. At high N, limited.

Subgrad refine:
```
def subgrad_refine(x,N,deadline,n_iter=5000):
    best_x=x.copy(); best_c=c5_anti
    x=best_x.copy(); c=best_c
    eta=0.01
    for _ in range(n_iter):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       cvec=correlate_anti(x,N); maxv=cvec.max()
       tol=max(1e-8, 5e-4*maxv)
       active=select_active(cvec, top_k=24, abs_tol=1e-8, rel_tol=5e-4)
       cvals=cvec[active]; m=cvals.max(); w=np.ones(len(active))/len(active)
       h=build_anti_h(x,N); dx=1/N; n=2N; g=zeros(n)
       for idx,wi in zip(active,w): ... same as active_grad but no logsum
       gx=g[:N]-g[n-1:N-1:-1]
       xnew=np.clip(x - eta*gx,0,1)
       cnew=c5_anti(xnew,N)
       if cnew < best_c - 1e-15:
           best_c=cnew; best_x=xnew.copy(); x=xnew; c=cnew; eta=min(eta*1.01,0.05)
       else:
           # if slightly worse but maybe direction, reduce eta and keep x? Maybe don't move.
           eta *= 0.97
           # optional: if cnew <= c + 1e-12: x=xnew
    return best_x,best_c
```

def x_candidates(N, seed, h_initial=None):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+N)
   cands=[]
   if h_initial is not None:
      x=full_to_anti(h_initial,N); cands.append(x)
      # binary threshold of that
      xt=np.where(x>0.5,1.0,0.0); cands.append(xt)
   cands.append(np.linspace(0,1,N))
   cands.append(np.linspace(1,0,N))
   for p in [1,2,3,5]:
      cands.append(np.clip(0.5 - 0.45*np.cos(2*np.pi*p*np.arange(N)/N + rng.rand()*2*np.pi),0,1))
   # random Fourier
   for _ in range(3):
      base=np.zeros(N)
      for p in range(1,8):
         base += rng.randn()*1.0/(p*0.5) * np.sin(2*np.pi*p*np.arange(N)/N + rng.rand()*2*np.pi)
      cands.append(np.clip(0.5 + 0.45*base/ (1+np.abs(base).max()+1e-12),0,1))
   # block binary
   for block in [7,15,31]:
      val=0; x=np.zeros(N)
      for i in range(N):
         if i%block==0: val=1-val
         x[i]=val
      cands.append(x)
   return cands
```
But running all at low N evaluate maybe okay. We should not spend too much generating. We can select top 3 by C.

`full_to_anti(h,N)`:
```
def full_to_anti(h,N):
    n_old=len(h); n_new=2*N
    if n_old == n_new:
       h_use=h
    else:
       dx_old=2.0/n_old; dx_new=2.0/n_new
       x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*dx_old
       x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*dx_new
       h_use=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
    x=0.5*(1.0 + h_use[:N] - h_use[n_new-1:N-1:-1])
    return np.clip(x,0,1)
```
Check slice `h_use[n_new-1:N-1:-1]` length N (start n-1 down to N). Good.
For direct T initial: `x_T = 1 - h0[n0-1:N0-1:-1]`. For initial average: as above.

def resample_x(x_old,N_new):
   if N_new==len(x_old): return x_old.copy()
   n_old=2*len(x_old)
   h=build_anti_h(x_old,len(x_old))
   return full_to_anti(h,N_new)
```

def exact_c5_from_h(h):

But adding binary hill increases code and time. We can add a simple version:
```
def binary_hill_climb(x,N,deadline,best_x=None,best_c=None):
    x=np.where(x>0.5,1.0,0.0)
    c=c5_anti(x,N)
    if best... update
    improved=True
    sweeps=0
    while improved and time<deadline:
       improved=False; sweeps +=1
       # try all flips once; if multiple improvements, take first? To speed choose best flip per sweep maybe.
       best_flip=-1; best_flip_c=c+1
       for i in range(N):
          if time>break
          x[i]=1.0-x[i]
          nc=c5_anti(x,N)
          x[i]=1.0-x[i]
          if nc < best_flip_c:
             best_flip_c=nc; best_flip=i
       if best_flip >=0 and best_flip_c < c - 1e-12:
          x[best_flip]=1-x[best_flip]; c=best_flip_c; improved=True
          if c<best_c: update
       else: break
    return x,best
```

def active_ls_polish(x,N,deadline,iters=2000):
  best_x=x.copy(); best_c=c5_anti
  x=best_x.copy(); c=best_c
  for it in range(iters):
    if time>deadline: break
    cvec=correlate_anti(x,N); maxv=cvec.max()
    active=select_active(cvec, top_k=8, abs_tol=1e-8, rel_tol=1e-4)
    vals=cvec[active]
    t=vals.mean()  # or 0.9*max +0.1*min?
    r=vals - t
    if np.max(np.abs(r))<1e-6: break
    # build J rows
    J=np.empty((len(active),N))
    for row, idx in enumerate(active): J[row]=single_lag_grad(x,N,int(idx))
    # solve dual
    G=J @ J.T
    lam=1e-6*trace? Add λ=1e-8*len? 
    try: z=np.linalg.solve(G + 1e-6*np.eye(r), r)
    except: z=np.linalg.lstsq(G,r,rcond=None)[0]
    delta=-J.T @ z
    # scale delta based on norm of movement
    dnorm=np.linalg.norm(delta)
    if dnorm <1e-14: continue
    delta /= dnorm
    improved=False
    for step in [0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005]:
       xnew=np.clip(x + step*delta,0,1)
       cnew=c5_anti(xnew,N)
       if cnew < c - 1e-15:
          x=xnew; c=cnew; improved=True; break
    if not improved:
       # maybe try -delta? balancing might need opposite? Test few
       for step in [0.01,0.005,0.002]:
          xnew=np.clip(x - step*delta,0,1)
          cnew=c5_anti(xnew,N)
          if cnew < c -1e-15: update; improved=True; break
    if improved and c < best_c: best update
    if not improved: break or continue with smaller?
  return best
```

```
def make_synth_cands(N, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    idx=np.arange(N)
    c=[]
    c.append(np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N))
    c.append(np.linspace(1.0,0.0,N))
    for p in (1,2,3,5):
       ph=rng.rand()*2*np.pi
       c.append(np.clip(0.5 - 0.49*np.cos(2*np.pi*p*idx/N + ph),0,1))
    # random Fourier 2
    for _ in range(2):
       base=np.zeros(N)
       maxamp=0.0
       for p in range(1,9):
          amp=rng.rand()/(p*0.7)
          base += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*p*idx/N + rng.rand()*2*np.pi)
          maxamp += amp
       if maxamp>0: base = base/maxamp
       c.append(np.clip(0.5 + 0.48*base,0,1))
    # block binary patterns: but binary maybe start; include 2
    for block in (9,17):
       x=np.zeros(N)
       val=0.0
       for i in range(N):
          if i % block ==0:
             val=1.0-val
          x[i]=val
       c.append(x)
    # half 0 half 1 with shuffle? maybe use rng permutation sorted to make clusters:
    xb=rng.rand(N)
    xbin=np.where(xb<0.5,1,0)
    c.append(xbin)
    return c
```

Let's draft final code in analysis, then ensure no syntax errors.

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from operator import itemgetter

def key_c(t):
    return t[0]

def get_initial_h():
    try:
        return np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        return None

def fft_len(L):
    if L <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()

def build_anti_h(x, N):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    h = np.empty(2 * N, dtype=float)
    h[:N] = x
    h[N:] = 1.0 - x[::-1]
    return h

def correlate_anti(x, N):
    n = 2 * N
    h = build_anti_h(x, N)
    b = 1.0 - h
    L = n - 1
    size = fft_len(L)
    f1 = np.fft.rfft(h[::-1], n=size)
    f2 = np.fft.rfft(b, n=size)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(f1 * f2, n=size)[:L]
    return corr * (1.0 / N)

def c5_anti(x, N):
    c = correlate_anti(x, N)
    return float(c.max())

def exact_c5_from_h(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return float((np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx).max())
```
Potential memory if h length 8192, full correlate uses 16383, okay.

`full_to_anti`:
```
def full_to_anti(h, N):
    n_new = 2 * N
    n_old = len(h)
    if n_old == n_new:
       hu = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    else:
       dx_old = 2.0 / n_old
       dx_new = 2.0 / n_new
       x_old = (np.arange(n_old) + 0.5) * dx_old
       x_new = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) * dx_new
       hu = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    flip = hu[n_new - 1 : n_new - 1 - N : -1]  # Need check stop.
```
For N=4, n_new=8, want indices 7,6,5,4. Slicing `hu[7:3:-1]` gives 7,6,5,4. `n_new - 1 - N = 8-1-4=3`. Good. For N=250, start499 stop249? length250, indices499..250? Wait n_new=500, N=250, want 499 down to250. Stop = 499-250 =249; `hu[499:249:-1]` length250 (499..250). Good. Formula `n_new - 1 - N` =249. Good.
`x = 0.5*(1.0 + hu[:N] - flip); return clip`.

`resample_x(x,N_new)`: if same return; else `full_to_anti(build_anti_h(x,N), N_new)`.

`select_active` as above. Need avoid set. We'll use boolean array for opp addition if active small.
```
def select_active(c, top_k=48, abs_tol=1e-7, rel_tol=5e-4):
    maxv = float(c.max())
    thresh = maxv - max(abs_tol, rel_tol * maxv)
    cand = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
    if cand.size == 0:
        cand = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    if cand.size > top_k:
        order = np.argsort(-c[cand])
        cand = cand[order[:top_k]]
    n = (len(c) + 1) // 2
    present = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=bool)
    present[cand]=True
    extras=[]
    for idx in cand:
        opp = 2*n - 2 - int(idx)
        if 0 <= opp < len(c) and not present[opp]:
            extras.append(opp)
    if extras:
        active = np.unique(np.concatenate((cand, np.array(extras, dtype=int))))
        cap = max(2*top_k, 32)
        if active.size > cap:
            active = active[np.argsort(-c[active])[:cap]]
    else:
        active = cand
    return active
```
`present` length up to 8191, fine.

`active_obj/grad`. We can add a helper `_softmax_weights(vals, alpha)` to avoid duplication:
```
def softmax_weights(vals, alpha):
    m = vals.max()
    sh = alpha * (vals - m)
    mx = sh.max()
    w = np.exp(sh - mx)
    ss = w.sum()
    if ss <= 0: w = np.ones_like(w)/w.size
    else: w /= ss
    val = m + (mx + np.log(ss)) / alpha
    return val, w
```
But objective only val; grad weights. Good.

`active_obj` uses it. `active_grad` uses it.

`single_lag_grad`:
```
def single_lag_grad(x,N,idx):
    n=2*N; h=build_anti_h(x,N); dx=1.0/N; center=n-1; k=int(idx)-center; g=np.zeros(n)
    if k>=0:
       nv=n-k; wm=dx
       g[0:nv] += wm*(1 - h[k:n]); g[k:n] += wm*(-h[0:nv])
    else:
       kk=-k; nv=n-kk; g[kk:n] += wm*(1 - h[0:nv]); g[0:nv] += wm*(-h[kk:n])
    return g[:N] - g[n-1:N-1:-1]
```
For k=0 works.

`active_grad` can use single_lag_grad per active weight? That would allocate and fold each time, slower but simpler. We'll use shared full grad as before for speed.

`active_grad` shared:
```
def active_grad(x,N,active,alpha):
    c=correlate_anti(x,N)
    vals=c[active]
    _,w=softmax_weights(vals,alpha)
    n=2*N; h=build_anti_h(x,N); dx=1.0/N; g=np.zeros(n); center=n-1
    for idx,wi in zip(active,w):
       if wi <= 1e-15: continue
       k=int(idx)-center; wm=wi*dx
       if k>=0:
          nv=n-k
          g[0:nv] += wm*(1.0-h[k:n])
          g[k:n] -= wm*h[0:nv]
       else:
          kk=-k; nv=n-kk
          g[kk:n] += wm*(1.0-h[0:nv])
          g[0:nv] -= wm*h[kk:n]
    return g[:N] - g[n-1:N-1:-1]
```
Use `g[k:n] -= wm*h[0:nv]` instead of += -; okay.

In shared, for k negative, `g[0:nv] -= wm*h[kk:n]`; correct.

`binary_hill_climb`:
```
def binary_hill_climb(x,N,deadline):
    x=np.where(x>0.5,1.0,0.0).astype(float)
    c=c5_anti(x,N)
    best_x=x.copy(); best_c=c
    for sweep in range(1000):
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       best_i=-1; best_cand=c+1e-15
       for i in range(N):
           if time.time() >= deadline: break
           x[i] = 1.0 - x[i]
           nc = c5_anti(x,N)
           x[i] = 1.0 - x[i]
           if nc < best_cand - 1e-15:
              best_cand=nc; best_i=i
       if best_i >=0 and best_cand < c - 1e-14:
           x[best_i] = 1.0 - x[best_i]
           c = best_cand
           if c < best_c:
              best_c=c; best_x=x.copy()
       else:
           break
    return best_x, best_c
```
This only first best improvement per sweep; if multiple, repeats. Good. Could be slow if N=1024 and 1000 sweeps; time check. Use in low N small.

`active_ls_polish`:
```
def active_ls_polish(x,N,deadline,iters=2000):
    x=x.copy(); c=c5_anti(x,N); best_x=x.copy(); best_c=c
    for it in range(iters):
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       cvec=correlate_anti(x,N)
       maxv=cvec.max()
       active=select_active(cvec, top_k=10, abs_tol=1e-8, rel_tol=1e-4)
       vals=cvec[active]
       if len(active) < 2:
           break
       r = vals - vals.mean()
       if abs(r).max() < 1e-7:
           # try subgrad? break
           pass
       J=np.empty((len(active), N))
       for row, idx in enumerate(active):
           J[row] = single_lag_grad(x,N,int(idx))
       G = J @ J.T
       lam = 1e-8 * max(1.0, float(np.trace(G)) / max(1,len(active)))
       try:
           z = np.linalg.solve(G + lam*np.eye(len(active)), r)
           delta = -J.T @ z
       except Exception:
           try:
              z = np.linalg.lstsq(G + lam*np.eye(len(active)), r, rcond=None)[0]
              delta = -J.T @ z
           except Exception:
              break
       dnorm = np.linalg.norm(delta)
       if dnorm < 1e-12: break
       delta /= dnorm
       jac_delta = J @ delta
       jnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(jac_delta))
       if jnorm > 1e-14:
          s0 = 0.003 / jnorm
       else:
          s0 = 0.01
       steps=[]
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
          steps.append(max(1e-6, min(0.2, s0*mult)))
       for neg in (False, True):
          for s in steps:
             if neg:
                xnew=np.clip(x - s*delta,0,1)
             else:
                xnew=np.clip(x + s*delta,0,1)
             cnew=c5_anti(xnew,N)
             if cnew < c - 1e-14:
                x=xnew; c=cnew
                if c < best_c:
                   best_c=c; best_x=x.copy()
                break
          if c != ...? Need flag.
```

`subgrad_refine`:
Use uniform active gradient. Adaptive step. We can allow moving if cnew <= c + 1e-12 to explore, but keep best. Let's implement:
```
def subgrad_refine(x,N,deadline,iters=5000):
  x=x.copy(); c=c5_anti; best_x=x.copy(); best_c=c
  eta=0.02
  for _ in range(iters):
    if time>=deadline: break
    cvec=correlate_anti(x,N)
    active=select_active(cvec, top_k=16, abs_tol=1e-8, rel_tol=5e-4)
    gx=uniform_active_grad(x,N,active)
    xnew=np.clip(x - eta*gx,0,1)
    cnew=c5_anti(xnew,N)
    if cnew < best_c -1e-15:
       best_c=cnew; best_x=xnew.copy()
    if cnew <= c + 1e-13:
       x=xnew; c=cnew; eta=min(eta*1.005,0.05)
    else:
       eta*=0.95
       # do not move
  return best_x,best_c
```
This can drift. If eta becomes tiny, break if eta<1e-6.

`project_feasible_full`:
```
def project_feasible_full(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
Use 100 iterations enough.

`active_obj_full`:
```
val=smooth; if mu>0: val += mu*(h.sum()-n/2)**2
```
`active_grad_full` similar no fold plus penalty.

Direct full phase details:
```
if h0 is not None:
   n0=len(h0)
   h_init=project_feasible_full(h0,n0)
   c_init = c5_full(h_init)  # or exact
   update
   if time < deadline - 500:
      end = min(time.time()+70, deadline-500)
      h_full,c_full=optimize_full(h_init,n0,end,alphas=[50,300,2000,10000,50000,200000],maxiter=600,mu=0.01,rounds=2)
      update
      # create anti fixed average from h_full if even
      if h_full.size%2==0:
         Nf=h_full.size//2
         xf=0.5*(1.0 + h_full[:Nf] - h_full[n-1:Nf-1:-1])
         hf=build_anti_h(np.clip(xf,0,1),Nf)

   n=len(h); L=2*n-1; size=fft_len(L); f1=rfft(h[::-1]); f2=rfft(1-h); corr=irfft(...); return corr*(2.0/n)

`optimize_full_lbfgs`:
```
def optimize_full_lbfgs(h,n,deadline,alphas,maxiter=400,mu=0.01,top_k=64,rounds=1):
    best_h=project_feasible_full(h,n); best_c=c5_full(best_h)
    cur_h=best_h.copy(); cur_c=best_c
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    for r in range(rounds):
      for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        c=correlate_full(cur_h); active=select_active(c, top_k)
        maxit=...
        try res=minimize(active_obj_full, cur_h, jac=active_grad_full, args=(n,active,alpha,mu), bounds=bounds, options...)
        except: continue
        hnew=project_feasible_full(res.x,n)
        cnew=c5_full(hnew)
        if cnew<best_c: update
        if cnew <= max(best_c, cur_c)+1e-12: cur_h=hnew; cur_c=cnew
        else: cur_h=best_h; cur_c=best_c
    return best_h,best_c
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def key_c(t):
    return t[0]


def get_initial_h():
    try:
        return np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        return None


def fft_len(L):
    if L <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()


def build_anti_h(x, N):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    h = np.empty(2 * N, dtype=float)
    h[:N] = x
    h[N:] = 1.0 - x[::-1]
    return h


def correlate_anti(x, N):
    n = 2 * N
    h = build_anti_h(x, N)
    b = 1.0 - h
    L = n - 1
    size = fft_len(L)
    f1 = np.fft.rfft(h[::-1], n=size)
    f2 = np.fft.rfft(b, n=size)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(f1 * f2, n=size)[:L]
    return corr * (1.0 / N)


def c5_anti(x, N):
    c = correlate_anti(x, N)
    return float(c.max())


def correlate_full(h):
    n = len(h)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = fft_len(L)
    f1 = np.fft.rfft(h[::-1], n=size)
    f2 = np.fft.rfft(1.0 - h, n=size)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(f1 * f2, n=size)[:L]
    return corr * (2.0 / n)


def c5_full(h):
    c = correlate_full(h)
    return float(c.max())


def exact_c5_from_h(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return float((np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx).max())


def project_feasible_full(h, n):
    target = n / 2.0
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(120):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def full_to_anti(h, N):
    n_new = 2 * N
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n_old = len(h)
    if n_old == n_new:
        hu = h.copy()
    else:
        dx_old = 2.0 / n_old
        dx_new = 2.0 / n_new
        x_old = (np.arange(n_old) + 0.5) * dx_old
        x_new = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) * dx_new
        hu = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    flip = hu[n_new - 1 : n_new - 1 - N : -1]
    x = 0.5 * (1.0 + hu[:N] - flip)
    return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)


def select_active(c, top_k=48, abs_tol=1e-7, rel_tol=5e-4):
    maxv = float(c.max())
    thresh = maxv - max(abs_tol, rel_tol * maxv)
    cand = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
    if cand.size == 0:
        cand = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    if cand.size > top_k:
        order = np.argsort(-c[cand])
        cand = cand[order[:top_k]]

    n_full = (len(c) + 1) // 2
    present = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=bool)
    present[cand] = True
    extras = []
    for idx in cand:
        opp = 2 * n_full - 2 - int(idx)
        if 0 <= opp < len(c) and not present[opp]:
            extras.append(opp)

    if extras:
        active = np.unique(np.concatenate((cand, np.array(extras, dtype=int))))
        cap = max(2 * top_k, 32)
        if active.size > cap:
            active = active[np.argsort(-c[active])[:cap]]
    else:
        active = cand
    return active


def softmax_smooth(vals, alpha):
    m = float(vals.max())
    sh = alpha * (vals - m)
    mx = float(sh.max())
    s = np.sum(np.exp(sh - mx))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return m, np.zeros_like(vals, dtype=float)
    val = m + (mx + np.log(s)) / alpha
    w = np.exp(sh - mx)
    w /= s
    return float(val), w


def active_obj(x, N, active, alpha):
    c = correlate_anti(x, N)
    vals = c[active]
    v, _ = softmax_smooth(vals, alpha)
    return v


def active_grad(x, N, active, alpha):
    c = correlate_anti(x, N)
    vals = c[active]
    _, w = softmax_smooth(vals, alpha)

    n = 2 * N
    h = build_anti_h(x, N)
    dx = 1.0 / N
    center = n - 1
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    for idx, wi in zip(active, w):
        if wi <= 1e-15:
            continue
        k = int(idx) - center
        wm = wi * dx
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            g[0:nv] += wm * (1.0 - h[k:n])
            g[k:n] -= wm * h[0:nv]
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            g[kk:n] += wm * (1.0 - h[0:nv])
            g[0:nv] -= wm * h[kk:n]

    return g[:N] - g[n - 1:N - 1:-1]


def active_obj_full(h, n, active, alpha, mu):
    c = correlate_full(h)
    vals = c[active]
    v, _ = softmax_smooth(vals, alpha)
    if mu > 0.0:
        v += mu * (h.sum() - n / 2.0) ** 2
    return float(v)


def active_grad_full(h, n, active, alpha, mu):
    c = correlate_full(h)
    vals = c[active]
    _, w = softmax_smooth(vals, alpha)

    dx = 2.0 / n
    center = n - 1
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    for idx, wi in zip(active, w):
        if wi <= 1e-15:
            continue
        k = int(idx) - center
        wm = wi * dx
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            g[0:nv] += wm * (1.0 - h[k:n])
            g[k:n] -= wm * h[0:nv]
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            g[kk:n] += wm * (1.0 - h[0:nv])
            g[0:nv] -= wm * h[kk:n]

    if mu > 0.0:
        g += 2.0 * mu * (h.sum() - n / 2.0)
    return g


def single_lag_grad(x, N, idx):
    n = 2 * N
    h = build_anti_h(x, N)
    dx = 1.0 / N
    center = n - 1
    k = int(idx) - center
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if k >= 0:
        nv = n - k
        g[0:nv] += dx * (1.0 - h[k:n])
        g[k:n] -= dx * h[0:nv]
    else:
        kk = -k
        nv = n - kk
        g[kk:n] += dx * (1.0 - h[0:nv])
        g[0:nv] -= dx * h[kk:n]
    return g[:N] - g[n - 1:N - 1:-1]


def uniform_active_grad(x, N, active):
    n = 2 * N
    h = build_anti_h(x, N)
    dx = 1.0 / N
    center = n - 1
    inv = 1.0 / max(1, len(active))
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for idx in active:
        k = int(idx) - center
        wm = inv * dx
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            g[0:nv] += wm * (1.0 - h[k:n])
            g[k:n] -= wm * h[0:nv]
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            g[kk:n] += wm * (1.0 - h[0:nv])
            g[0:nv] -= wm * h[kk:n]
    return g[:N] - g[n - 1:N - 1:-1]


def iter_cap(n):
    if n <= 650:
        return 800
    if n <= 1100:
        return 650
    if n <= 2600:
        return 480
    if n <= 5200:
        return 320
    return 230


def optimize_lbfgs(x, N, deadline, alphas, maxiter=400, top_k=48, rounds=1):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_c = c5_anti(best_x, N)
    cur_x = best_x.copy()
    cur_c = best_c
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * N

    for _ in range(max(1, int(rounds))):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 8:
                break
            remaining = deadline - time.time()
            if remaining < 15:
                break

            c = correlate_anti(cur_x, N)
            active = select_active(c, top_k=top_k)
            mi = min(int(maxiter), iter_cap(2 * N))
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    active_obj, cur_x, jac=active_grad,
                    args=(N, active, float(alpha)),
                    method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                    options={'maxiter': mi, 'maxfun': mi * 5,
                             'ftol': 1e-20, 'gtol': 1e-15,
                             'maxls': 50, 'maxcor': 50}
                )
                xnew = np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0)
            except Exception:
                continue

            cnew = c5_anti(xnew, N)
            if np.isfinite(cnew) and cnew < best_c:
                best_c = cnew
                best_x = xnew.copy()
            if np.isfinite(cnew) and cnew <= max(best_c, cur_c) + 1e-12:
                cur_x = xnew.copy()
                cur_c = cnew
            else:
                cur_x = best_x.copy()
                cur_c = best_c

    return best_x, best_c


def optimize_full_lbfgs(h, n, deadline, alphas, maxiter=400, mu=0.02, top_k=64, rounds=1):
    h = project_feasible_full(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_full(best_h)
    cur_h = best_h.copy()
    cur_c = best_c
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    for _ in range(max(1, int(rounds))):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 8:
                break
            remaining = deadline - time.time()
            if remaining < 15:
                break
            c = correlate_full(cur_h)
            active = select_active(c, top_k=top_k)
            mi = min(int(maxiter), iter_cap(n))
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    active_obj_full, cur_h, jac=active_grad_full,
                    args=(n, active, float(alpha), float(mu)),
                    method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                    options={'maxiter': mi, 'maxfun': mi * 5,
                             'ftol': 1e-20, 'gtol': 1e-15,
                             'maxls': 50, 'maxcor': 50}
                )
                hnew = project_feasible_full(res.x, n)
            except Exception:
                continue

            cnew = c5_full(hnew)
            if np.isfinite(cnew) and cnew < best_c:
                best_c = cnew
                best_h = hnew.copy()
            if np.isfinite(cnew) and cnew <= max(best_c, cur_c) + 1e-12:
                cur_h = hnew.copy()
                cur_c = cnew
            else:
                cur_h = best_h.copy()
                cur_c = best_c

    return best_h, best_c


def binary_hill_climb(x, N, deadline):
    x = np.where(np.asarray(x, dtype=float) > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0).astype(float)
    c = c5_anti(x, N)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_c = c

    for _ in range(20000):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        best_i = -1
        best_cand = c + 1e-13
        for i in range(N):
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            old = x[i]
            x[i] = 1.0 - old
            nc = c5_anti(x, N)
            x[i] = old
            if nc < best_cand - 1e-14:
                best_cand = nc
                best_i = i
        if best_i >= 0 and best_cand < c - 1e-14:
            x[best_i] = 1.0 - x[best_i]
            c = best_cand
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_x = x.copy()
        else:
            break
    return best_x, best_c


def subgrad_refine(x, N, deadline, iters=5000):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    c = c5_anti(x, N)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_c = c
    eta = 0.02

    for _ in range(int(iters)):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        if eta < 1e-7:
            break
        cvec = correlate_anti(x, N)
        active = select_active(cvec, top_k=18, abs_tol=1e-8, rel_tol=5e-4)
        gx = uniform_active_grad(x, N, active)
        xnew = np.clip(x - eta * gx, 0.0, 1.0)
        cnew = c5_anti(xnew, N)
        if np.isfinite(cnew) and cnew < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = cnew
            best_x = xnew.copy()
        if np.isfinite(cnew) and cnew <= c + 1e-13:
            x = xnew
            c = cnew
            eta = min(eta * 1.005, 0.05)
        else:
            eta *= 0.95

    return best_x, best_c


def active_ls_polish(x, N, deadline, iters=800):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    c = c5_anti(x, N)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_c = c

    for _ in range(int(iters)):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break

        cvec = correlate_anti(x, N)
        active = select_active(cvec, top_k=10, abs_tol=1e-8, rel_tol=1e-4)
        vals = cvec[active]
        if len(active) < 2:
            break
        r = vals - vals.mean()
        if float(np.max(np.abs(r))) < 1e-8:
            break

        J = np.empty((len(active), N), dtype=float)
        for row, idx in enumerate(active):
            J[row] = single_lag_grad(x, N, int(idx))

        G = J @ J.T
        tr = float(np.trace(G)) / max(1, len(active))
        lam = 1e-8 * max(1.0, tr)
        try:
            z = np.linalg.solve(G + lam * np.eye(len(active)), r)
            delta = -J.T @ z
        except Exception:
            try:
                z = np.linalg.lstsq(G + lam * np.eye(len(active)), r, rcond=None)[0]
                delta = -J.T @ z
            except Exception:
                break

        dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(delta))
        if dnorm < 1e-12:
            break
        delta /= dnorm

        jd = J @ delta
        jnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(jd))
        if jnorm > 1e-14:
            s0 = 0.003 / jnorm
        else:
            s0 = 0.01

        steps = []
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05):
            steps.append(max(1e-6, min(0.2, s0 * mult)))

        improved = False
        for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
            for s in steps:
                xnew = np.clip(x + sign * s * delta, 0.0, 1.0)
                cnew = c5_anti(xnew, N)
                if np.isfinite(cnew) and cnew < c - 1e-14:
                    x = xnew
                    c = cnew
                    if c < best_c:
                        best_c = c
                        best_x = x.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break
        if not improved:
            break

    return best_x, best_c


def make_synth_cands(N, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + 777)
    idx = np.arange(N, dtype=float)
    cands = []

    cands.append(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N))
    cands.append(np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, N))

    for p in (1, 2, 3, 5):
        ph = rng.rand() * 2.0 * np.pi
        cands.append(np.clip(0.5 - 0.49 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * p * idx / N + ph), 0.0, 1.0))

    for _ in range(2):
        base = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
        amp_tot = 0.0
        for p in range(1, 9):
            amp = rng.rand() / (p * 0.7)
            base += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * p * idx / N + rng.rand() * 2.0 * np.pi)
            amp_tot += amp
        if amp_tot > 0.0:
            base /= amp_tot
        cands.append(np.clip(0.5 + 0.48 * base, 0.0, 1.0))

    for block in (9, 17):
        xb = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
        val = 0.0
        for i in range(N):
            if i % block == 0:
                val = 1.0 - val
            xb[i] = val
        cands.append(xb)

    cands.append(np.where(rng.rand(N) < 0.5, 1.0, 0.0))
    return cands


def make_random_binary(N, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + 31337)
    return np.where(rng.rand(N) < 0.5, 1.0, 0.0)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 30.0

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1.0
    best_n = 0

    h0 = get_initial_h()

    if h0 is not None and len(h0) > 0:
        n0 = len(h0)
        h_init = project_feasible_full(h0, n0)
        c_init = c5_full(h_init)
        if np.isfinite(c_init) and c_init < best_c:
            best_c = c_init
            best_h = h_init.copy()
            best_n = n0

        if time.time() < deadline - 400:
            full_end = min(time.time() + 85.0, deadline - 350.0)
            if full_end > time.time() + 15.0:
                try:
                    hf, cf = optimize_full_lbfgs(
                        h_init, n0, full_end,
                        alphas=(50.0, 300.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0),
                        maxiter=520, mu=0.02, top_k=80, rounds=1
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(cf) and cf < best_c:
                        best_c = cf
                        best_h = hf.copy()
                        best_n = len(hf)
                    if len(hf) % 2 == 0 and len(hf) > 2:
                        Nf = len(hf) // 2
                        xf = full_to_anti(hf, Nf)
                        hfa = build_anti_h(xf, Nf)
                        cfa = c5_anti(xf, Nf)
                        if np.isfinite(cfa) and cfa < best_c:
                            best_c = cfa
                            best_h = hfa.copy()
                            best_n = len(hfa)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    low_N = 256
    low_end = min(time.time() + 185.0, deadline - 360.0)

    if time.time() < low_end:
        low_cands = []
        if best_h is not None:
            low_cands.append(full_to_anti(best_h, low_N))
        low_cands.extend(make_synth_cands(low_N, seed))

        evaluated_low = []
        for x in low_cands:
            x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
            c = c5_anti(x, low_N)
            if np.isfinite(c):
                evaluated_low.append((c, x))
        if evaluated_low:
            evaluated_low.sort(key=key_c)
            for c, x in evaluated_low:
                if c < best_c:
                    best_h = build_anti_h(x, low_N).copy()
                    best_c = c
                    best_n = 2 * low_N

        hill_end = min(time.time() + 55.0, low_end - 120.0)
        if hill_end > time.time() + 8.0 and evaluated_low:
            evaluated_low.sort(key=key_c)
            for jj in range(min(3, len(evaluated_low))):
                if time.time() >= hill_end:
                    break
                x0 = np.where(evaluated_low[jj][1] > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0)
                xb, cb = binary_hill_climb(x0, low_N, min(time.time() + 12.0, hill_end))
                if np.isfinite(cb):
                    evaluated_low.append((cb, xb))
            xr = make_random_binary(low_N, seed)
            xb, cb = binary_hill_climb(xr, low_N, min(time.time() + 12.0, hill_end))
            if np.isfinite(cb):
                evaluated_low.append((cb, xb))
            evaluated_low.sort(key=key_c)

        if evaluated_low:
            evaluated_low.sort(key=key_c)
            top_low = []
            for _, x in evaluated_low[:3]:
                top_low.append(x)
            opt_end = min(time.time() + 125.0, low_end)
            per = max(20.0, (opt_end - time.time()) / max(1, len(top_low)))
            for x in top_low:
                if time.time() >= opt_end:
                    break
                xend = min(time.time() + per, opt_end)
                if xend < time.time() + 8:
                    break
                try:
                    xo, co = optimize_lbfgs(
                        x, low_N, xend,
                        alphas=(20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0),
                        maxiter=720, top_k=64, rounds=2
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(co) and co < best_c:
                        best_c = co
                        best_h = build_anti_h(xo, low_N).copy()
                        best_n = 2 * low_N
                except Exception:
                    pass

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = build_anti_h(np.full(256, 0.5), 256).copy()
        best_c = exact_c5_from_h(best_h)
        best_n = len(best_h)

    mid_N = 1536
    mid_end = min(time.time() + 230.0, deadline - 170.0)
    if time.time() < mid_end - 20:
        x_mid = full_to_anti(best_h, mid_N)
        h_mid = build_anti_h(x_mid, mid_N)
        c_mid = c5_anti(x_mid, mid_N)
        if np.isfinite(c_mid) and c_mid < best_c:
            best_c = c_mid
            best_h = h_mid.copy()
            best_n = len(h_mid)
        try:
            xo, co = optimize_lbfgs(
                x_mid, mid_N, mid_end,
                alphas=(50.0, 500.0, 5000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0),
                maxiter=460, top_k=44, rounds=1
            )
            if np.isfinite(co) and co < best_c:
                best_c = co
                best_h = build_anti_h(xo, mid_N).copy()
                best_n = len(best_h)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if budget_s >= 950:
        high_N = 4096
    else:
        high_N = 2048

    if time.time() < deadline - 80:
        x_high = full_to_anti(best_h, high_N)
        h_high = build_anti_h(x_high, high_N)
        c_high = c5_anti(x_high, high_N)
        if np.isfinite(c_high) and c_high < best_c:
            best_c = c_high
            best_h = h_high.copy()
            best_n = len(best_h)

        high1_end = min(time.time() + 300.0, deadline - 85.0)
        if high1_end > time.time() + 20:
            try:
                x_high, ch = optimize_lbfgs(
                    x_high, high_N, high1_end,
                    alphas=(50.0, 500.0, 5000.0, 50000.0),
                    maxiter=330, top_k=36, rounds=1
                )
                if np.isfinite(ch) and ch < best_c:
                    best_c = ch
                    best_h = build_anti_h(x_high, high_N).copy()
                    best_n = len(best_h)
            except Exception:
                pass

        high2_end = min(deadline - 45.0, time.time() + 230.0)
        if high2_end > time.time() + 18:
            try:
                x_high2, ch2 = optimize_lbfgs(
                    x_high, high_N, high2_end,
                    alphas=(50000.0, 200000.0, 800000.0),
                    maxiter=300, top_k=28, rounds=1
                )
                if np.isfinite(ch2) and ch2 < best_c:
                    best_c = ch2
                    best_h = build_anti_h(x_high2, high_N).copy()
                    best_n = len(best_h)
                    x_high = x_high2
            except Exception:
                pass

        if time.time() < deadline - 25:
            try:
                xsg, csg = subgrad_refine(x_high, high_N, deadline - 18, iters=2500)
                if np.isfinite(csg) and csg < best_c:
                    best_c = csg
                    best_h = build_anti_h(xsg, high_N).copy()
                    best_n = len(best_h)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if time.time() < deadline - 8:
            try:
                xls, cls = active_ls_polish(x_high, high_N, deadline - 4, iters=450)
                if np.isfinite(cls) and cls < best_c:
                    best_c = cls
                    best_h = build_anti_h(xls, high_N).copy()
                    best_n = len(best_h)
            except Exception:
                pass

    final_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float).copy()
    final_n = len(final_h)

    if final_n > 0 and abs(final_h.sum() - final_n / 2.0) > 1e-8:
        final_h = project_feasible_full(final_h, final_n)

    try:
        final_c = exact_c5_from_h(final_h)
    except Exception:
        if final_n % 2 == 0 and final_n >= 2:
            xf = full_to_anti(final_h, final_n // 2)
            final_h = build_anti_h(xf, final_n // 2)
            final_c = c5_anti(xf, final_n // 2)
        else:
            final_c = best_c

    if not np.isfinite(final_c):
        final_c = best_c
        final_h = project_feasible_full(final_h, final_n)

    return final_h, float(final_c), int(final_n)
```
